West Coast gasoline imports hit five-year high after
refineries went dark: CAS

India emerges as third-largest fuel supplier in 2026

Import sources expand from 11 to 15 nations

Mexico receives 65% of regional fuel exports

    Nearly a year after Phillips 66 permanently shut its 139,000
b/d Wilmington, California refinery, the US West Coast fuel market
has undergone a structural shift in its import sourcing and export
patterns, with gasoline imports reaching their highest level in at
least five years and the supplier base expanding to 15 countries

                                                                   progressed. While CARBOB dominated the initial response to

                                                                   remained elevated even as CARBOB volumes fell sharply in later

                                                                   barrels while CARBOB imports were zero, indicating a greater
